- **redis-online-store-failed-error-wrongt-c8fe8dc6404bc4b6**: Delete or rename the colliding key so the feature key holds the hash type Feast wrote, and prefix feature keys so application keys cannot collide with them again, because the read failed on a key that already exists in Redis holding a different value type than the feature hash the reader expects. Use when this exact failure blocks the work described in the title. Not for a vendor wide outage or a different error message. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/redis-online-store-failed-error-wrongt-c8fe8dc6404bc4b6))
- **redis-oom-command-not-allowed-when-use-2a4097ad416c1441**: Shows which Redis keys to evict first when Redis rejects writes at its memory cap. Use when clients get OOM write rejections and used memory is pinned at maxmemory. Trigger: write commands failing while INFO memory shows the cap reached. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/redis-oom-command-not-allowed-when-use-2a4097ad416c1441))
- **redis-oom-command-not-allowed-when-use-34b28ee761f7f74c**: Responds to Redis rejecting writes because memory exceeded maxmemory. Use when write commands fail with the OOM error and the instance is otherwise healthy. Not for Redis being down, replication or persistence failures, or read errors. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/redis-oom-command-not-allowed-when-use-34b28ee761f7f74c))
- **redis-oom-command-not-allowed-when-use-fec991030b70ae90**: Fixes Redis OOM errors when used memory exceeds maxmemory. Use when writes fail with OOM command not allowed, when an agent caches without TTLs, or when eviction policy blocks writes. Not for connection failures, for slow commands, or for replication lag. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/redis-oom-command-not-allowed-when-use-fec991030b70ae90))
- **redis-oom-diagnose-with-info-memory-an-c87f6fe1260b9fc1**: Redis OOM: diagnose with INFO memory and bigkeys before raising maxmemory. Do not restart Redis unless forced: it causes a full cache cold start. Use when hitting this exact issue with Redis OOM. Not for unrelated errors or different features. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/redis-oom-diagnose-with-info-memory-an-c87f6fe1260b9fc1))
